# Claudius → Rust: port plan

Goal: **full parity** with the TypeScript app, ending in a pure-Rust Claudius.
This file is the source of truth for the port: strategy, module map, what is
done, what is missing, and the rules that keep the two implementations
interchangeable while they coexist.

## Strategy: backend first, library first, UI last

A big-bang rewrite would mean months with nothing to test against. Instead:

1. **Port the backend as libraries** (`claudius-agent`, `claudius-core`) with
   no HTTP or UI assumptions, so the same core can later power a native UI.
2. **Put a Rust HTTP server in front** (`claudius-server`). It serves the same
   HTTP/SSE API as `app/api/**` for every ported route and **reverse-proxies
   everything else** (pages, assets, the HMR websocket, unported API routes)
   to the existing Next.js server.
3. **Use the unchanged React UI and the Playwright suite as the parity
   oracle.** If the real UI works against the Rust backend, the port is right.
   Every route ported moves traffic from Next to Rust with no UI change.
4. When the API is fully ported, Next only serves the UI. Then **replace the
   UI with a native Rust one** (last phase, below).

```text
 browser ──► claudius (Rust, :3100) ──┬─► ported routes ─► claudius-core ─► claudius-agent ─► `claude` CLI
                                     │                     (sessions, SQLite,     (stream-json +
                                     │                      settings, JSONL)       control protocol)
                                     └─► everything else ─► Next.js (:3000) — pages, assets, HMR, unported API
```

## Crates

| Crate | Size | What it is | TS counterpart |
|---|---|---|---|
| `claudius-agent` | ~4.3k lines | Host side of the Claude Agent SDK. There is **no official Rust SDK**, so this re-implements `query()`: it spawns the native `claude` binary with stream-json NDJSON and speaks the bidirectional control protocol (`control_request`/`response`, `can_use_tool`, `hook_callback`, `mcp_message`, elicitation, `control_cancel_request`, and redelivery of the prompts an `initialize` reply lists). Includes in-process SDK MCP servers and hooks. Ships `claudius-fake-claude`, a scriptable fake CLI for tests; it appends each `$FAKE_CLAUDE_*` log line in one write, so all the fakes of one server can share a log (I22). Both ends read stream-json lines with `js::parse_line` (`JSON.parse`, I17). Closing a CLI runs the SDK's `ProcessTransport.close()` timer (R13): stdin ended, SIGTERM after 2 s, SIGKILL 5 s later. Once the server is exiting (`begin_exit`), SIGTERM comes at once and SIGKILL 2 s later. | `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk` (`sdk.mjs`) |
| `claudius-client` | ~14.3k lines | The native UI's chat state machine: `use-session.ts` as plain Rust over the server's events, with no UI toolkit or transport (U01). U01a: the hook's pure helpers, the `types.ts` shapes they build and the `sdk-message-filters.ts` predicates; JS strings and numbers as on the server side (`claudius_core::js_string`). U01b: the pure modules the reducer calls (task status and provisional rows, idle sweeps, prompt queues, system-entry coalescing, stream-recovery backoff, the boot guard over a `TabStorage` trait, `parse-init`, effort-wait, workflow `meta`, scheduled-loop stops, and the informational / hook-pill / reset-trigger labels). They reuse core's `cost_from_tokens`, `parse_task_list_result`, fast-mode and `session_loops` ports. U01c: the frame that the later slices fill in. `ChatState` (`state.rs`) holds every hook cell and the refs the handlers read. `apply(&mut ChatState, ServerEvent, &mut dyn ClientEnv) -> Vec<Effect>` (`apply.rs`) runs one SSE message, and fetch replies and timer fires come back in by id (`fetch_settled` / `timer_fired`). `Effect` (`effect.rs`) covers fetches, timer set / clear, window events, URL `replaceState` and bad-payload logs. `ServerEvent` (`event.rs`) has a variant for every type the server sends; unknown types are kept raw. The events so far are `ready`, `holder_changed`, `error`, `cwd_changed`, `session_title`, `goal_changed`, account / agent / mode, `tips`, `feedback_survey`, the four nudges, the MCP notices, the recap pair, `turn_status` and `replay_done`, plus the 30 s poll and `refreshSessions` (`refresh.rs`) and `requestRecap` / `dismissRecap`. U01m: the frame uses U01b's modules instead of its own copies: the prompt queues are `Vec<Prompt>` (`impl Keyed`) through `merge_server_prompts`, the idle sweep is `idle_reconcile`, the effort wait is `EffortWaitState`, the skipped boot goes through `scope_skipped_boot`, and `ChatState` holds `TaskInfo`, `ScheduledLoop`, `ApiRetryState` and `InitInfo`'s lists. U01d: the `sdk` event's message and stream assembly (`sdk.rs`). `assistant` folds each split into the bubble of its message id (`upsert_assistant_split`), with the rate-limit / Opus-high-demand / model-unavailable and error tags, the per-call usage estimate and the rail's thinking / tool rows; a subagent split goes to its Task's trail. `stream_event` keeps a per-message delta scratch (`DeltaScratch`), merged into the streaming bubble after every block event. `result` ends the turn (`pending`, `lastTurnCompletedAt`, the idle sweep, the trailing bubble), refreshes the session list, and raises the fast-mode edges and the resume / startup-failure / budget / compaction notices. `prompt_suggestion` keeps the last four chips. U01e (`user_events.rs`): `user` (a `tool_result` lands on its tool_use in the transcript and every subagent trail, closes the tool's progress frame, rail row and recent edit, settles its Task, seeds a Workflow's provisional task, and names or backgrounds a shell; other records become a subagent's prompt, the compaction divider, slash / CLI-wrapper / reminder pills, or a user bubble with images and a peer sender, after the synthetic filters), `tool_progress`, `system` / `thinking_tokens`, and a tool_use's Edit / MultiEdit / Write (`recentEdits`), background Bash and KillBash (`backgroundBashes`) side effects. The todo / loop side effects are U01g's. U01f (`prompts.rs`): the three prompt FIFOs (`permission_request`, `ask_user_question`, `mcp_elicitation_request`, through `prompt_queue`), `prompt_settled`, `plan_approval_request`, and the `resolvePermission` / `resolveElicitation` / `submitAskAnswer` / `resolvePlan` / `setPermissionMode` actions (an `Effect::Fetch` after the optimistic change; an uncaught rejection is `Effect::UnhandledRejection`). U01i (`status.rs`): `usage_snapshot` (a wholesale spread over the estimate), `plan_usage` / `plan_usage_unavailable` (and the subscription-type ref), `effort_applied`, `model_changed` (the chat-command / auto, fast-mode and auto-mode-off notices) and `advisor_disabled_on_model_change`. Actions: `setModel` (a rejection reverts to the server's model and raises the notice), `setEffort` (the "may wait" note through `effort_wait`), `setUltracode`, `setFast`, `setAdvisorModel`, `reEnableAdvisor`, `setAgent` and the `dismiss*` notices. Each is an optimistic change plus a POST. U01h (`system_events.rs`): every other `system` subtype (`init` through `parse_init`, `hook_*` and the running-hook indicator, `status`, `compact_boundary` and the failed-`/compact` notice armed by `slash_invoked`, `model_fallback`, `api_retry`, `permission_denied`, informational, `worker_shutting_down`, `commands_changed`, `elicitation_complete`, `model_refusal_*`, the fast-credits `notification`, `local_command_output` and the catch-all pill), `conversation_reset`, `rate_limit_event` (one pill per limit type), `session_snapshot`, and the `loadOlder` / `jumpToUuid` actions (a transcript page through `synthesize_older` / `with_paged_compact_boundary`; a jump's outcome is an `Effect::ActionResolved`). U01g (`task_events.rs`): `task_snapshot` (persisted tasks and trails filling the gaps, and stand-ins for pills compaction removed, after paging back), the `task_*` subtypes and `background_tasks_changed`, `todos_auto_cleared`, the todo / loop side effects of tool calls and results (TodoWrite / TaskCreate / TaskUpdate / TaskList, CronCreate / CronDelete, ScheduleWakeup), and the `clearTodos` / `updateTodoItem` / `dismissTodosAutoCleared` actions (failures as `Effect::ConsoleWarn`, `updateTodoItem`'s outcome as an `Effect::ActionResolved`). U01n (`account.rs`): `moveToActiveAccount` (no state; its `{ ok, error }` is an `Effect::ActionResolved`) and `effortChangeWouldWait` (a pure read through `nextEffortWait`). U01j (`queue.rs`): `queue:updated` (the queue mirror and `sdkQueuedTurns`), `send` (the optimistic pending bubble and badges, rolled back on a failure or a `queued` reply, and the error banner), `enqueue`, `cancelQueued` / `editQueued` / `reorderQueued` / `sendQueuedNow` / `sendAllQueuedNow`, `interrupt` (the "N queued messages will still run" pill), `takeOver`, `renameTitle` and `setGoal` / `clearGoal` (optimistic, and reverted to the value the call started from), and `submitFeedback`. Every promise-valued action (`jumpToUuid`, `updateTodoItem`, `moveToActiveAccount`, `send`, `enqueue`, `editQueued`, `renameTitle`, `setGoal`, `clearGoal`, `submitFeedback`) ends with one `Effect::ActionResolved { action, outcome }`, the promise's value as the TS builds it (the convention is in `effect.rs`). U01k: the session lifecycle in `lifecycle.rs` / `stream.rs`: boot and the boot guard, create / switch / bind, the stream as `OpenEventSource` / `CloseEventSource` effects plus three host inputs, recovery, the reconnect badge, visibility, the hook's commit effects, "Open anyway", the refresh action; the legacy queue migration isn't ported. | `lib/client/use-session.ts`, `lib/client/types.ts`, `lib/client/sdk-message-filters.ts`, `lib/shared/rate-limit-prefixes.ts` , `lib/shared/events.ts`, `lib/client/{task-status,idle-reconcile,prompt-queue,system-entries,stream-recovery,api-retry,boot-guard}.ts`, `lib/shared/{parse-init,effort-wait-note,stop-self-paced-loop,workflow-meta,system-informational,hook-events,slash-commands}.ts` |
| `claudius-transport` | ~2.4k lines | The native UI's I/O host (U02): the `Transport` trait, `InProcessTransport` (`claudius_server::app` + `tower` oneshot, no sockets) and `RemoteTransport` (hyper-util, `http://`), `fetch`-faithful requests with the `claudius.workspace` cookie jar, a WHATWG `EventSource` emulation, and the `Driver` that runs a `ChatState`'s effects (fetches, timers, streams; U02d) and hands the UI's to a callback. | browser `fetch` / `EventSource`, `lib/client/use-session.ts`'s host side |

### Module map (claudius-core)

| Rust | Ports |
|---|---|
| `session.rs` | `lib/server/session.ts`: start/resume (`start()` and `build_options` audited against the TS in S27, `tests/session_start_golden.rs`; the spawn-failure tail in S27a; replay / resync `at` through `js_date::date_parse` in S27c), `consume`, broadcast + replay buffer (`broadcast()`, `captureSnapshotState()`, `captureTaskState()` and `consume()` audited against the TS in S28, `session/tests/stream_golden.rs` and `tests/session_consume_golden.rs`), subscribe order, `sendInput` (reminders, ultrathink, images, slash), queue (with CC 2.1.316's Stop holds, R14b), `canUseTool` (tool budgets, permissions, AskUserQuestion, ExitPlanMode), elicitation, mode/model, goals, holder, usage folding, interrupt, `resyncFromDisk` and the JSONL watcher glue, advisor. |
| `session_consume.rs` | The pure parts of session.ts `consume()` (S28): `set_model_target` (the `/model` echo, `/^set\s+model\s+to\s+(\S+)/i` with JS `\s`, ASCII-only `/i` and `String()` coercion), `queued_turns` (`queued_turn_count`, a JS number), and `plan_usage_event` with `normalize_extra_usage` (`lib/shared/plan-usage.ts`): the `plan_usage` event a successful turn's `get_usage` reply becomes, `None` where the TS IIFE throws. `Session::spawn_plan_usage` sends it, or `plan_usage_unavailable`. |
| `session/live_init.rs` | session.ts `broadcast()`'s hooks on a live (not replayed) `system/init`, run from `Session::broadcast` so that a `dev-broadcast` init gets them too (S28a): the MCP startup notices before the fan-out, then `enforceAdvisorModelCompatibility(<init model>)` and `refreshAppliedEffort`. The advisor check is decided in place and cleared on its own task, without the consume loop awaiting it (S28b). The model stays raw. Replayed against the TS in `claudius-server/tests/session_init.rs`. |
| `session/create_options.rs` | `POST /api/sessions`'s untyped option reads (S27d). `from_create_body` reads the merged body as `new Session` / `start()` do: `typeof number`, truthiness flags with `persistSession === false`, `${}` string options, iterated `additionalDirectories` and the raw sandbox `allowedDomains`. It also covers the `start()` throws that make create a 500 (`planModeInstructions.trim`, no primitive form, `model?.includes` while the advisor is armed), and hands back the raw `model` / `agent` / `fallbackModel`. Replayed against the TS route in `claudius-server/tests/session_create.rs`. |
| `session/input.rs` | session.ts `sendInput()`'s reads of the raw input (S29): the slash command's name and args (JS `trim` / `\s`), the `!images || images.length === 0` text branch, the `[Image #n]` tokens (ASCII digits read with `Number`, each image inlined once, `-0` is `0`), images without a numeric `ordinal` appended (a string by code point), and V8's TypeErrors for a `null` element, a non-iterable `images`, or a `text` that isn't a string (`text.trim` / `text.toLowerCase is not a function`, raised after the turn is marked running; S29a). `uuid_or_new` is `opts.uuid ?? randomUUID()`: a uuid of any type is kept (S29a). `Session::send_input_value` takes `text` and `images` as the body holds them and returns the throw; `send_input` is its typed wrapper. |
| `jsonl_watch.rs` | The file-watching half of `startJsonlWatcher` / `stopJsonlWatcher` / `scheduleJsonlResync`: a `notify` watch on the transcript's directory, filtered to `<id>.jsonl`, coalesced by a 200 ms trailing debounce that re-arms while a resync is running. The session starts it on a detached thread (FSEvents can take seconds to start), so `start()` doesn't wait, and dropping it cancels the debounce at once and closes the OS watcher on a detached thread (S01b). |
| `session_manager.rs` | `session-manager.ts` + `session-resume.ts`: create with resume dedupe (a `start()` that throws, on a `null` user settings.json (D10a) or when the SDK's `query()` rejects its options (S27: a fallback model equal to the model), fails the create with the error's `name` and message, is ended, and isn't left for a later create to join), idle reaper (`CLAUDIUS_SESSION_IDLE_REAP_MS`; it re-arms instead of reaping a session with a pending prompt or an armed loop), `getOrResume`, last recorded permission mode, and the in-place rebuilds `recover_in_place` / `restart_in_place` (same id and settings, at most 3 thinking-replay attempts in a row; S19), and `restart_if_dead` / `dead_session_response` for a CLI that died between turns (R03, CC 2.1.311). The same-id rebuild is shared by concurrent callers and allowed 3 times in a row, then `max_attempts`; the budget refills after 5 min or a successful turn. Passive callers (`get_or_resume`) get only the first attempt, and only after a live init. A failed rebuild keeps the dead session bound. `list()` is registration order (a JS `Map`: a failed rebuild re-registers last); `reap_ms_from` reads `CLAUDIUS_IDLE_REAP_MS` with `Number()` (past 2^31 - 1 fires after 1 ms, like Node's timer); `last_recorded_permission_mode` reads lossy UTF-8 with `JSON.parse` (S29). |
| `events.rs` | `lib/shared/events.ts`: `shouldBufferEvent`, replay window selection, ask-question parsing, `buildAskUpdatedInput`, session snapshot, spinner tips. |
| `spinner_tips.rs` | session.ts's spinner-tip settings: `normalizeSpinnerTipsOverride`, `mergeSpinnerTipsFile` (`~/` expansion like `path.join`, absolute only, regular file ≤ 256 KiB, lossy UTF-8, JSON array) and the `spinnerTipsConfig` behind the `tips` event, read once at start. Replayed against the TS, `tests/spinner_tips_golden.rs`. |
| `sessions_db.rs` | `sessions-db.ts` (with `getSessionTitle`'s legacy `session-titles.json` fallback and its row migration, and `getSessionTitlesByCwd`: per-cwd probes in chunks of 500, the cross-workspace fan-out, the legacy shadow read with JS property lookup), `retitleIndexedSession` (CC 2.1.309: the non-live rename's row update in every known workspace, with a read-only probe first; it never creates a DB), `session-usage-db.ts`, `prompt-drafts-db.ts`, `goal-messages-db.ts`, `suggested-messages-db.ts`. `recordSuggestedMessage` / `recordGoalMessage` take the posted uuid and text of any type. A falsy uuid records nothing; anything else binds by `sql_text::js_args` (S29a). |
| `queue_db.rs` | `queued-messages-db.ts`. `images_json` is `JSON.stringify` of the posted `images` whenever its `length` is positive (integer keys first), read back only when it's an array (S29). `uuid` and `text` are JS values of any type, bound by `sql_text::js_args`: a queued `7` is stored as `"7.0"`, and an array spreads. The row `enqueue_tail` returns keeps them as passed. A uuid stored as NULL reads back as `null` (S29a). Migration 026's `held_by_stop` (CC 2.1.316): `hold_all` / `release_holds`, and `pop_head(.., unless_held)` leaves a held head for `start()`'s drain; `pop_by_uuid` and a plain `pop_head` still take it (R14a). The session side (R14b): a Stop mid-turn holds, `start()`'s drains skip held rows, and a successful turn releases them. |
| `read_paths.rs` | `lib/shared/read-tool-paths.ts` (`extractReadPaths`) and the floored Node `mtimeMs` that `reseedReadPathsAfterCompact` sends with each `seed_read_state`; the session keeps `recent_read_paths` and re-seeds after each `compact_boundary` (S18). |
| `reminders.rs` | `system-reminders.ts`: `wrapReminder`, and a `ReminderQueue` that is one channel (`queueReminder` / `takePendingReminders`, or the mid-turn pair), FIFO, no dedupe, `None` when empty; the debug-only kind isn't kept. The linter-modified-file reminder: session.ts `postWriteSnapshots` as an `IndexMap` in first-write order, `post_write_target` for the `PostToolUse` capture, `flushLinterModifiedReminder` and `linterModifiedReminderBody`; sha256 via `sha2`. The date-change body: `localDateKey`, `toDateString` and `dateChangeReminderBody`. The hook and the flush call site live in `session.rs`. Replayed against the TS in `tests/reminders_golden.rs` (S21). |
| `thinking_replay.rs` | `lib/server/thinking-replay-recovery.ts`: `isThinkingReplayErrorText`, `extractMessageText`, `thinkingReplayErrorFrom` and `planThinkingReplayRecovery` (anchor on the latest assistant with thinking, else the latest assistant; replay the last real prompt before it; resume at the last assistant before that). The session schedules one recovery per session object on the synthetic 400 and the manager rebuilds it in place (S19). |

The run is a single turn (`permissionMode: "auto"`, `permissionPrompts: "none"`) with its record, a live buffer (`subscribe_run`, 5 s linger) and the `on_run_finished` hook. The query is a literal: each UTF-16 unit goes through V8's non-`u` `i` Canonicalize (the unit's `toUpperCase` if that is one unit and doesn't take a non-ASCII unit to ASCII), and a KMP over canonical units finds the first match in linear time, with no regex engine. "100")`, 100 when that is 0 or NaN, clamped to 1–200 and truncated. 8 workers read the files through `Limits::read_utf8` (node 22's `readFileHandle`: a regular file past 2 GiB is refused unread, a text past V8's `(1 << 29) - 24` UTF-16 units is refused, and either skips the file; `usage_seed` and `asset_ingest` read through it too, I27, and `cost_aggregate`'s transcripts and cache, I29); the answer is the first `limit` matches in scan order. ## Running it

```sh
# terminal 1 — the existing app (UI + not-yet-ported routes)
bun run dev                                  # :3000

# terminal 2 — the Rust front door; open http://localhost:3100
cd rust && cargo run -p claudius-server -- --port 3100 --next http://127.0.0.1:3000
```

Flags and environment variables:
- `--host` / `CLAUDIUS_RS_HOST` (default `127.0.0.1`)
- `--port`, `-p` / `CLAUDIUS_RS_PORT` (default `3100`)
- `--next URL` / `CLAUDIUS_NEXT_URL` (default `http://127.0.0.1:3000`), or `--no-next` to run without a proxy
- `--claude-bin PATH` / `CLAUDIUS_CLAUDE_BIN`. Otherwise the binary is found the same way the SDK finds it: the bundled `node_modules/@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk-<platform>/claude`, then `claude` on `PATH`.
- `CLAUDIUS_DEV=1|0` forces the dev-only `dev-*` test routes on or off. Without it they follow TS: off when `NODE_ENV=production`, otherwise on in debug builds and off in release builds. The parity e2e config sets `CLAUDIUS_DEV=1`.
- `CLAUDIUS_DEV_FETCH_BASE=URL` (only with the dev routes on): every outbound request of the server's HTTPS client (`claudius_core::fetch`) goes to `URL/<host>/<path>` instead, for a local fake of the Anthropic endpoints. Outbound requests use `HTTP_PROXY` / `HTTPS_PROXY` / `NO_PROXY` only with `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1`, as node 22's fetch does.
- `CLAUDIUS_STATE_HOME=DIR` moves Claudius's own state (`~/.claude/.claudius`, per-project DBs, workspaces) to `DIR` while the CLI keeps the real `$HOME` and login. Use it for an isolated test world that still talks to the real model.
- The scheduler runs Claudius's jobs, and Rust answers the schedule routes by default (G07d). `--no-scheduler` / `CLAUDIUS_SCHEDULER=0|false|no|off` hands them to Next; `--scheduler` forces it on. Start the Next behind it with `CLAUDIUS_RUST_SCHEDULER=1` (`playwright.rust.config.ts` and `live-smoke.sh` do), or both fire every job.

**Open the UI through the Rust port (:3100), not :3000.** Live sessions exist
only in the process that started them (see below).

### Logging

Logs go to stdout through `tracing`. The default filter is `claudius=info`, a target *prefix*, so it covers every crate (`claudius_core`, `claudius_agent`, …) and the TS-parity targets below. Set `RUST_LOG` to replace it; an invalid `RUST_LOG` falls back to the default. Colours appear only when stdout is a terminal.

| Target | TS equivalent | Level |
|---|---|---|
| `claudius::sess_load` | `[sess-load] …` (`sessLoadDebug()`) | debug |
| `claudius::session` | `[session] …`, `[session.*] …` | warn |
| `claudius::api` | `[api/*] …`, `[input] …` | warn / error |
| `claudius::scheduler` | `[scheduler] …` | error; `armed` / `disarmed` at debug (Rust-only) |
| `claudius_agent::query` | (SDK internals) spawn argv, control traffic | debug |

Messages keep the TS bracket prefix and field names, so the same grep works on both backends.

```sh
CLAUDIUS_DEBUG_SESSIONS=1 cargo run -p claudius-server   # the TS switch: adds claudius::sess_load=debug
RUST_LOG=claudius=info,claudius::sess_load=debug …        # same, explicitly
RUST_LOG=claudius=warn …                                  # quiet: only warnings / errors
RUST_LOG=claudius=info,claudius_agent=debug …             # + CLI spawn argv and control-request traces
```

## Coexistence rules

- **Live sessions live only in the Rust process.** Every `/api/sessions/:id/*`
  route is answered by Rust. Unported ones return **501**
  `{error, notPorted: true, sessionId}` rather than being proxied: if Next
  received them, it would resume a *second* CLI for the same session id.
- **The disk-only collection routes are Rust's too:** `/api/sessions/all` and
  `/api/sessions/{export,file,info,transcript}/**` (D19), `/api/sessions/fork` (D05).
  Nothing under `/api/sessions` reaches Next.
- **Routes outside `/api/sessions` that use `sessionManager` are proxied but
  degrade.** Next's in-memory session manager is empty while Rust owns the
  sessions, so these see no live sessions: `schedule` (session loops).
  Porting them is phase 3.
- **Boot tasks run in one process only** (G12a, G12b). `instrumentation.ts`'s customization upgrade check, workspace reconcile and `updaterScheduler.boot()` run in Next while Next serves beside Rust. `claudius --boot-tasks` (or `CLAUDIUS_BOOT_TASKS=1`) runs them in Rust before the listener binds, for a Rust-only server; it is off by default until the N section retires Next. The updater keeps its own `CLAUDIUS_UPDATER_DISABLED=1` gate.
- **Scheduled jobs run in one process only.** Rust answers the schedule routes and the run stream by default; with `--no-scheduler` they are forwarded to Next unchanged. `session-loops` (+ `cancel`) are always Rust's. Next skips `boot()` / `arm()` under `CLAUDIUS_RUST_SCHEDULER=1`.
- **On-disk formats are shared and must stay byte-compatible:** the same
  SQLite schema (embedded migrations), the same settings files, the same CLI
  JSONL. Either backend can open what the other wrote.

## Status

As of 2026-11-17: **all 183 routes are served by Rust**; none is Next-only, proxied or answers 501. Per-route status is generated into **[ROUTES.md](ROUTES.md)** by
`rust/scripts/route-coverage.sh`. The `route_coverage` test fails if it goes stale, or if a Rust route
misses methods its TS route exports (axum would answer 405 instead of falling through to Next).

### Ported routes (all 46 `/api/sessions/:id/*`; see ROUTES.md for the per-route list and counts)

- **Collection:** `GET`/`POST /api/sessions`, `POST /api/sessions/rename`, `GET /api/sessions/search` (D20: full-text search over one workspace's transcripts; literal, case-insensitive), `GET`/`PUT /api/sessions/open-tabs` (the persisted tab strip, scoped by the customization / workspace cookies), `GET /api/sessions/peer-source` (G11: who sent a cross-session peer message, for the chat's link; `hostedHere` from the Rust session manager), `POST /api/sessions/fork` (D05: the SDK fork plus the optional `/fork` worktree)
- **Stream and turns:** `stream` (SSE replay protocol), `input`, `interrupt`, `stop-task`, `background-task` (Ctrl+B), `bash` (`!` mode)
- **Prompts:** `permission`, `ask-answer`, `plan`, `elicitation`, `pending-prompts`
- **Session settings:** `mode` (GET/POST), `model` (GET/POST), `effort` (POST), `fast` (POST), `ultracode` (POST), `output-style` (GET/PATCH), `agent` (GET/POST), `agents` (GET), `advisor` (GET/POST), `goal`, `holder`
- **Queue:** `queue/send-all`, `queue/[uuid]` (DELETE), `queue/[uuid]/move`, `queue/[uuid]/send-now`
- **Todos:** `clear-todos` (POST), `todos/[itemId]` (POST); both resume a reaped session
- **Info:** `commands`, `context` (with `weeklyUsedSkills` on the full fetch), `denials`, `usage-behaviors`
- **Transcript:** `transcript` (a page older than a cursor), `search` (literal, case-insensitive)
- **Recap:** `recap` (202; the recap or its skip reason rides SSE)
- **MCP:** `mcp-dynamic` (POST: replace the session's SDK-added MCP servers; the change rides the next turn as the `mcp-delta` reminder)
- **Notifications:** `notification-prefs` (GET/POST: the per-session block / snooze row; the bus reads it (G01a); sessions (S31) and `notifications/dev-emit` feed the bus)
- **Notifications (G01b, G01c, `claudius_server::notifications`):** `notifications` (GET: one workspace's rows, newest first, or `workspace=all`), `notifications/counts` (GET), `notifications/[id]/read`, `read-all`, `read-by-kind`, `read-by-session` (POST), `notifications/dev-emit` (POST, dev-only), `notifications/stream` (GET: the SSE). Sessions (S31) and `dev-emit` feed the bus.
- **Rewind:** `rewind` (POST: put the files the session edited back as they were at a user message, or `dryRun: true` to preview what would change)
- **Composer/chat bind state:** `prompt-draft` (GET/PUT/DELETE), `prompt-color` (GET/POST), `goal-messages`, `suggested-messages`
- **Dev-only test routes** (behind `claudius_server::dev`; when off they refuse like TS with 404 / 404 / 403 / 404): `dev-broadcast`, `dev-reap`, `dev-inject-denial`, `notifications/dev-emit`. `dev-broadcast` reaches the notification bus through `Session::broadcast` (S31).
- **Global (phase 3):** `mcp` (GET/POST), `mcp/[name]` (DELETE), `mcp/[name]/toggle`, `mcp/[name]/reconnect` (POST; G03): a trusted cwd's MCP config files, and one live session's `mcp_status` / `mcp_toggle` / `mcp_reconnect` through the Rust session manager
- **Agents (G05, `claudius_server::agents`):** `agents` (GET: one file or both scopes' listings; PUT: write, then reload the cwd's live sessions), `agents/[name]` (DELETE, same reload), `agents/db` (GET/PUT/DELETE: the `db_agents` rows a session gets as `Options.agents` at start)
- **Plugins (G04, `claudius_server::plugins`):** `plugins` (GET: each scope's plugin settings for a trusted cwd, plus one live session's installed plugins through `reload_plugins` held on cache impact, and its init load errors; POST: toggle, marketplace add-extra / remove-extra / remove-policy, plugin-config options; a scope file that doesn't load is the 422), `plugins/available` (GET: the cloned marketplaces' plugins by installs), `plugins/reload` (POST: `reload_plugins` on one live session; `?force=1` lifts the hold)
- **Accounts (G02b `claudius_server::accounts_oauth`, G02c `claudius_server::accounts`):** `accounts/oauth` (POST: `{action: "start"}` answers the authorize URL; `{action: "complete", flowId, code, label}` exchanges the pasted code and stores a new `oauth-token` profile, dropping its id from the profile cache); `accounts` (GET: public state; POST: add; PATCH: auto-rotate / switch the active profile, then every live session recomputes its drift; DELETE `?id`); `accounts/profile` (GET `?id&refresh=1`: the profile lookup through the shared 5-minute cache); `account` (GET `?sessionId`: a live session's `initialize` account).
- **Memory (G08, `claudius_server::memory`):** `memory/auto` (GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE: a trusted cwd's auto-memory files and `MEMORY.md` index; each write that lands queues the `memory-update` reminder on every live session in that cwd), `memory/rules` (GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE: user or project rule files)
- **Models (G06, `claudius_server::models`):** `models` (GET: the oldest live session's `supportedModels()`, or the static list, curated by the user's `modelPicker`), `models/probe` (GET: one single-turn query per pinned model, all at once, on the sessions' CLI)
- **Filesystem (D04):** `fs/dirs` (GET/POST: the workspace form's folder picker, confined to `$CLAUDIUS_FS_ROOT` or the home dir, with the macOS TCC heads-up gate), `fs/list` (GET: the composer's `@`-mention candidates under a trusted cwd)
- **Assets (D17, `claudius_server::assets`):** `GET /api/assets` (the Files page's list: `scope`, `type`, `q`, `limit`, `cursor`; `backfill=1` first ingests the project's transcript images), `GET`/`DELETE /api/assets/[hash]` (the stored bytes with the row's media type, `Content-Length` and `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`; delete removes the file and the row), `GET /api/assets/[hash]/uses`, and `GET /api/local-image?path=` (an image file from the OS temp dirs only, typed by extension, at most 10 MiB, `Cache-Control: private, max-age=10`). `input` records a prompt's images into the store (`recordSendUses`).
- **Health (D26a, `claudius_server::heartbeat`):** `heartbeat` (GET/HEAD: liveness, no I/O), `heartbeatz` (GET: the server cwd's database opens, migrates and answers `SELECT 1`; 503 with `openDb`'s message). Next's `vary`, OPTIONS 204 and empty 405/400 are mirrored.
- **Splash (D26b, `claudius_server::splash_examples`):** `splash-examples` (GET/PUT/DELETE: the active cwd's splash chips and greeting-name override, plus the account-derived fallback name; scoped by the customization / workspace cookies). Next's `vary`, OPTIONS 204 and empty 405/400 are mirrored.
- **Workspace git (D06, `claudius_server::git`):** `GET /api/workspaces/:id/git/status` (a `cust_…` id runs on the customization's mirror), `git/diff?path&mode`, `git/show?path&ref`. The other git routes are D07–D09's.
- **Workspace branches (D07, `claudius_server::git_branches`):** `GET …/git/branches`, `GET …/git/branch-compare?mode&base&head` (the commit / diffstat report, or `mode=diff` against the working tree), `POST …/git/checkout`, `POST …/git/branch-rename`, `POST …/git/branch-delete` (local `-d` / `-D`, or `remote: true` → `push <remote> --delete`). A `cust_…` id is looked up as a workspace, as in TS.
- **Workspace commit (D08, `claudius_server::git_commit`):** `POST …/git/stage`, `POST …/git/commit`, `GET`/`POST`/`DELETE …/git/commit-draft`, `POST …/git/commit-message`. A `cust_…` id is looked up as a workspace, as in TS.
- **Docker (D23, `claudius_server::docker`):** `GET /api/docker/containers` (the Docker page's 5 s poll): `docker ps` and `docker stats --no-stream` run together and are joined by short id. Always 200; `status: "unavailable"` with `docker-not-installed` / `docker-daemon-down` / `unknown` when docker is missing, the daemon is down or `ps` fails.
- **Workspaces (D01, `claudius_server::workspaces`):** `GET`/`POST /api/workspaces`, `GET`/`PATCH`/`DELETE /api/workspaces/[id]`, `POST …/[id]/select` (the `claudius.workspace` cookie), `POST …/reorder`, `GET`/`POST …/[id]/icon`, `GET …/[id]/roots`
- **Workspace relocation (G09, `claudius_server::move_sessions`):** `GET /api/workspaces/[id]/move-sessions` (the "move sessions to the new root?" preview: sessions recorded under the root, live ones mid-turn, other workspaces on the same root) and `POST` (re-root the workspace and carry its transcripts, sidecars, `assets/` and `.claudius.db` rows along; 409 while a live session there is busy, idle ones are ended first)
- **Schedule (G07b, `claudius_server::schedule`; while Rust runs the scheduler (the default), else proxied to Next):** `GET`/`POST /api/schedule`, `GET`/`PATCH`/`DELETE /api/schedule/[id]`, `POST …/[id]/run-now` (202 once the run ends), `GET …/[id]/runs?limit` (running runs with `attached`). `session-loops` (+ `cancel`) and `runs/[runId]/stream` are G07c's. POST/PATCH validate `effort`, and a `cwd` / `enabled` PATCH clears the skip reason (R01).

- **Account pin (S30, `claudius_server::session_account`):** `GET`/`POST /api/sessions/:id/account` (the profile a live session runs under; POST moves it to the active account).
- **Sessions collection (D19, `claudius_server::sessions_collection`):** `sessions/all`, `sessions/info/[id]`, `sessions/export/[id]`, `sessions/transcript/[id]`, `sessions/file/[id]` (DELETE). Only `sessions/fork` (D05) is still proxied.
- **Schedule, live half (G07c, `claudius_server::schedule_live`):** `GET …/[id]/runs/[runId]/stream` (while Rust runs the scheduler), `GET /api/schedule/session-loops`, `POST …/session-loops/cancel`. A cancel on a dead session is 410 (R01).
- **Workspace host (D02, `claudius_server::workspace_roots`):** `GET …/[id]/roots`, `POST …/[id]/reveal`, `POST …/[id]/shell`.
- **Files (D03a/b, `claudius_server::workspace_files`):** `GET/POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE /api/workspaces/:id/files`. `files/preview/[...path]` is D03c's (below).
- **Git sync (D09, `claudius_server::git_sync`):** `POST …/git/merge`, `rebase`, `pull-merge`, `remote`; `GET …/git/remote-host`, `pr-status`.
- **Settings (D10, `claudius_server::settings_routes`):** `GET /api/settings`, `settings/full` (GET/PUT), `permissions` (GET/POST), `auto-mode` (GET/POST/DELETE), `additional-dirs` (POST), `skill-overrides` (GET/POST).
- **Settings backup (D11, `claudius_server::settings_bundle`):** `GET /api/settings/export` (the attachment), `POST /api/settings/import` (JSON, or the `file` field of `multipart/form-data`), `GET`/`DELETE /api/settings/import/[id]`, `POST …/[id]/resolve`.
- **Skills, hooks, keybindings, CLAUDE.md (D12, `claudius_server::skills_hooks`):** `skills` (GET/PUT), `skills/[name]` (DELETE), `hooks` (GET/POST/DELETE), `hooks/disabled` (POST), `keybindings` (GET/PUT), `claudemd` (GET/PUT).
- **Customizations store (D13, `claudius_server::customizations`):** `GET /api/customizations` (its POST is D14's), `[id]` (GET, which since N03a adds `srcDir`, the mirror the customization chat runs in; PATCH/DELETE), `[id]/select`, `[id]/publishes`, `[id]/diff`.
- **Customization lifecycle (D14, `claudius_server::customization_actions`):** `POST /api/customizations` (the bootstrap), `[id]/sync` (GET/POST), `[id]/description` (GET/POST/PATCH), `[id]/build-app` (GET/POST), `[id]/publish`, `[id]/auto-fix`, `[id]/deactivate` and `[id]/publishes/[pubId]/revert`.
- **Customization preview (D15, `claudius_server::customization_preview`):** `GET`/`POST`/`DELETE /api/customizations/[id]/preview` and `POST …/preview/restart`; `[id]` DELETE stops the preview in-process.
- **Doctor (D22, `claudius_server::doctor`):** `GET /api/doctor`, `POST /api/doctor/fix`.
- **Worktrees (D05, `claudius_server::worktrees`):** `GET /api/worktrees?cwd` (a trusted cwd's `git worktree list --porcelain`, parsed) and `POST /api/sessions/fork`.
- **Community (D24, `claudius_server::community`):** `community/prefs` (GET/PUT: the consent and nickname in `~/.claude/settings.json`), `community/admin/check` (GET), `community/admin/[...path]` (GET/POST/DELETE: the moderation proxy to the chat server's `/admin/*`, `X-Admin-Token` added server-side, `?client=` appended; 503 without a chat server, 403 without a token, Next's empty 500 for an unreachable upstream or a status `new Response` refuses).
- **Feedback (G10, `claudius_server::feedback`):** `POST /api/feedback` (forwarded to the CLI through the live session, then the local row — stored even when the forward is refused, with the refusal `reason` in the answer) and `GET /api/feedback` (the `sessionId`'s cwd, newest first, `limit` at most 500).
- **Pages (N02, `claudius_server::pages`):** the router's fallback. The workspace cookie and the bare-path, root, `welcome` and customization-chat redirects that `proxy.ts` and the redirect-only server components answer today; everything else goes to the Next proxy. Since N03a the customization chat is a client page that redirects an unknown id itself (`router.replace` on a 404), so Next alone and soft navigations behave the same.
- **Limits and prices (D18a, `claudius_server::limits` / `cost_prices`):** `limits` (GET `?cwd`; PUT the non-negative caps; POST `{action: audit|override}`) and `cost/refresh-prices` (GET the cache-or-bundle status; POST fetches now, 200 / 502 / 503).
- **Cost (D18b, `claudius_server::cost`):** `GET /api/cost` and `GET /api/cost/loops` (`?cwd`, 400 `unknown cwd`, a throw is a 500 `{error}`), and `GET /api/cost/overview` (one shared in-flight run). Priced with `cost_prices::pricing`, so a refresh-prices POST shows at once.
- **Customize (D16, `claudius_server::customize`):** `GET`/`PUT /api/customize-settings`, `GET /api/customize/runtime`.
- **File preview (D03c, `claudius_server::files_preview`):** `GET /api/workspaces/:id/files/preview/*path`.
- **Heapdump (D29, `claudius_server::heapdump`):** `POST /api/heapdump` answers 501 `{ok: false, error}`. The TS route writes Node's diagnostic report for the Next process, and the Rust backend has no Node runtime to report on.
- **Updater (D27a, D27b, `claudius_server::updater`):**
  - `updater/settings`: GET, and PUT (400 for a bad `mode`).
  - `updater/status` (GET): the settings, the state minus a `pending` that HEAD already contains, the install's git facts and the runtime mode.
  - `updater/log` (GET `?offset`), and `updater/check` (POST, single flight).
  - `updater/changelog` (GET `?from&to`): the GitHub compare through `AppState::https`, 400 / 502.
  - `updater/apply` (POST, D27b): one apply at a time (a second call gets the run in flight's outcome); only an own `allowCcMerge: true` in a JSON object body is the click. Heal, check, pull (ff-only / stash-ff / cc-merge), safety-net heal, install + build, restart (`claudius_core::updater::{apply, restart}`). The scheduler is ported; `main.rs` boots it with `--boot-tasks` (G12b).

### Verification

- `cargo test --workspace` runs 1200 tests (gate b1117e, 2026-11-17, every merge through U03a / I14d / I27 / I29 / U01m / U01d / U01f), all passing. Earlier: 1190 (gate b1117d, before I29 / U01f), 1149 (gate b1117c, through U01b / U01c / I14c), 1052 tests in gate b1115i (2026-11-15, through R10a), where two failed under load ~350, both explained: `memory` (I22's log splicing) and `workspace_files` › `read` (`ConnectionReset`, I24). The load flakes seen in other gates are `bash_golden` truncation/long, `docker` (4 MiB buffer cases) and `accounts_routes` (health timeout); I24's resets are fixed. Seen once, not reproduced: `memory_golden` › `module_scenarios_match_the_ts` step 16 (gate b1117b, 2026-11-17), where `scandir` of a path with a ~330-byte component gave EINVAL instead of ENAMETOOLONG; the same binary then passed 22 runs. A gate started under `nohup` fails `workspace_roots` step 335 (`kill -HUP $$` exits 0, because the ignored SIGHUP is inherited); start gates as plain background jobs.
  - Session tests drive the fake CLI through subscribe order, permission / ask / plan round trips, queue drain, `end()` draining prompts, mode/model/interrupt with restricted coercion, resume-from-transcript replay, external transcript writes reaching subscribers (S01), applied effort (init seed, picks saved or session-only, model-switch refresh, re-emit on subscribe, stale replies dropped; S02), the agent switch (no-op without a query, a refused apply still recorded; S06), the to-do snapshot driven by scripted TaskCreate / TaskUpdate / TaskList turns, the mid-turn reminder through a real PreToolUse callback, the stale-TodoWrite nudge (S07a), `clearTodos` (the cutoff that bounces racing writes, the durable `todosClearedAt` marker, the empty snapshot, the toast only for non-manual clears of a non-empty list) and the resume path (the marker seeds the cutoff; the 24h stale and all-completed auto-clears; S07b), the user's per-item edits (kept until the model touches the id live, moved on promotion, re-applied over a TaskList rebuild and on resume, stored in JS key order; S07c), the turn-end sync (the `todos-current` reminder after every successful turn with open items, the stale flag at 3 turns without progress and the clear at 6, the all-done clear with its one-turn TaskList reprieve; S07d), subagent persistence (the row from `task_started`, the inner conversation inside its write window, the terminal state, and `task_snapshot` on a live session and on one rebuilt from disk after a reap; none without tasks; S08), Ctrl+B (`background_tasks` with the CLI's `task_updated` patch moving `turn_status` to idle with one background task; S09), scheduled loops (a live CronCreate promoted to its cron id and a quiet wake-up with its tick and usage, then stopped and deleted; a resumed transcript's loops at their JSONL times, and a resync replaying an external stop; S11a), the keep-alive (an unwatched session with an armed wake-up outlives the idle reaper, an idle one doesn't, and `stop: true` lets it go; S11c), a spinner-tips `tipsFile` reaching a subscriber's `tips`, read once at start (S22), the post-compaction re-seed (S18), and CLI crash reporting.

Recorded clocks, ids and `nextRunAt` slots are matched against the real clock. 4 more tests cover who answers (with `CLAUDIUS_SCHEDULER=0`, requests are forwarded to Next byte for byte and nothing is written or armed; with it, G07c's paths are still forwarded; with neither, the proxy's 404) and a run-now whose client disconnects still finishing. `tests/schedule_attached.rs` subscribes to a live run in process: `attached` is true while watched, false unwatched, and absent once the run has ended.
  - `!` mode (S10): `tests/bash_golden.rs` replays the TS `BashSession` scenarios against the Rust shell (plus a timeout killing the whole process group, and the 1.5 s SIGKILL escalation on timeout and dispose, R07); `claudius-server/tests/bash.rs` replays the TS route on 39 recorded bodies, the broadcast rows and the next prompt's prefix, and checks that ending the session kills the shell.
  - The JSONL watcher's debounce, busy re-arm, stop and error paths run on tokio's paused clock; `tests/jsonl_watch.rs` (server) appends to a live session's transcript and reads the line off its open SSE stream. `session_fake` waits for the off-thread start (S01b) and checks that a session ended while its watch is still starting never installs it.
  - One HTTP end-to-end test covers the whole front door: proxy fallback, 501 guard, create validation, SSE headers and order, input → turn, permission with a queued message, mode/model/effort/fast/ultracode/output-style/agent/holder/goal, clear-todos (live, unknown id, and a reaped session it resumes), todos/[itemId] (422s, percent-decoded ids, a reaped session resumed and edited), drafts, colors, advisor auto-disable on a model switch.
  - Transport (I24): `claudius-server/tests/unread_body.rs` pins the drain layer on the real binary: after an early 405 from axum or `heartbeat::other_method`, a keep-alive connection serves the next request and a `Connection: close` one ends with a FIN once the body is in; the 1 MiB / 5 s bounds; `Expect: 100-continue` untouched; proxied requests reach Next unchanged. It also pins `common::read_response` against a local resetting server (a reset after a complete response is its end; before, a failure). Server tests share `tests/common`'s transport: one write per request, `free_port` refusing a port a leaked listener holds, `wait_healthy` reporting a lost bind with the server's stderr, and the fake CLI built once per process.
- **Live check, 2026-10-25:** the unchanged React UI ran through the Rust front door against the real `claude` binary. It sent a prompt and rendered the reply, replayed the transcript after a reload, and completed a permission card (Allow once → Bash ran → turn finished). The page load made no 4xx/5xx API calls.
- Clippy is clean.
- **Parity e2e, latest (2026-11-23, after N03b)** (`rust/parity/e2e-status.md`): **465 passed, 1 failed, 7 skipped** of 473 on Rust (230 spec files, 11.2 min), and **465 / 1 / 7** on TS over the same tree (17.0 min). The one failure on each is `cc-parity-2.1.222-diff-raw-content` (`pre-existing`, TODO I25). S34's run just before it: Rust 459 / 2 / 7 of 468. Live smoke (I05, S34) on the real CLI: the API half passed 7 runs out of 7, the UI half 6 out of 7 (the one miss is I37).
- **Performance (2026-11-23)**: production Next 16.4.6 against the release `claudius`, run one at a time on the same data (35 real transcripts, 245 MB, the largest 107 MB; a clone of the repo). The report is `rust/target/par/status/perf-2026-11-23.html`.
  - **Rust wins:**
    - start-up: 49 ms against 0.99 s;
    - idle memory: 12 MB against 164 MB;
    - requests per second with many clients: 5–7× TS's;
    - faster on 8 of the 11 requests timed (up to 5×; search 206 ms against 463);
    - a tiny request while big transcripts load: p95 0.2 ms against TS's 39 ms.
  - **Rust loses:**
    - memory held after heavy use: 2.4 GB against 1.5 GB;
    - the 107 MB transcript: 635 ms against 509 for the full load, 636 against 468 for its last 50 messages;
    - the warm cost overview: 57 ms against 17.

    Those losses are section 5P (F01–F07), and F07 sets the memory budget below TS's.
  - **In the first shippable shape** (Rust in front, Next for pages), launch and idle memory don't improve until N04–N05. The report's analysis: merge the code with Rust off now to stop the drift (R14), switch users after R14 + 5P + I37, and N04–N05 deliver the launch and memory wins.

The task specs (`sdk-update-0.3.273-task-notification-worker-restart`, `…-0.3.257-task-resource-links`, …) mock the stream, so they can't reach the Rust `task_snapshot`; and a user-facing instance of the app held `.next-e2e`. Live check instead: the React UI through the Rust front door (`localhost:3100`) created a session and drove the real CLI.

## Known gaps (parity debt)

**Session engine (`session.rs`)**
- JSONL watcher (S01): turns another client writes to the transcript reach open streams live. Differences from TS:
  - Deliberate: it watches the directory the CLI writes the transcript to (`<config>/projects/<SDK name>`, where `resyncFromDisk` reads it). TS watches `projectRoot(cwd)` (`homedir()/.claude/projects/<encodeProjectDir>`), which is the same directory, also through a profile's symlinked `projects/`, except for cwds over 200 encoded chars (the SDK hashes the name) or a `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` with its own `projects/`. There the TS watcher never fires.
  - As in TS, only the cwd's directory is watched (a transcript `resyncFromDisk` finds under the realpath or a git worktree's directory isn't), and a directory that doesn't exist yet at `start()` means no watcher for that session.
  - One OS watcher, and with it one `notify` thread, per live session. libuv shares one inotify instance per process, so on Linux the default `max_user_instances` (128) caps live watchers lower than in TS; past it, sessions run without one, as on any watch error.
- Subagent (Task) persistence: at parity (S08). `captureTaskState` / `persistTask` / `persistTaskForToolUse` / `sendTaskSnapshot` match the TS on recorded scenarios (`tasks_golden_capture`: write throttle, inner-message cap, shared tool-use ids, ambient / background counts), and the database layer on recorded rows (`tests/tasks_golden.rs`). Differences:
  - The capture keeps only the SDK's own types (string, number, boolean fields; a string `parent_tool_use_id`). TS assigns whatever arrives, so e.g. a numeric `description` would reach its row as text through SQLite's TEXT affinity; here it's dropped.
  - Writes run synchronously under the session lock; TS fires them and moves on (same order either way).
  - A text value in a number column (only a foreign writer could put one there) reads back as no number, or as a set flag, instead of TS's raw string; the rest of the list still loads.
- Todos: at parity (S07a–d): the capture, `clearTodos` with `POST /:id/clear-todos`, the `todosClearedAt` resume seed and start-time auto-clears, the user's per-item overrides with `POST /:id/todos/[itemId]`, and the turn-end sync with the progress-based stale flag. Override persistence runs synchronously under the session lock where TS fires it and moves on; the TS 503 for a Session instance that predates `updateTodoItem` (a dev-HMR artifact) has no Rust counterpart. Small differences: a `null` list entry counts as open and never matches an id, where TS throws reading `.status` / `.id` off it (out of `broadcast()`, out of `start()` or `updateTodoItem` when an override or edit meets one, and out of the turn-end sync, whose rejection TS swallows); TS's write-only `todosTouchedThisTurn` isn't kept.
- Reminders (S21): at parity on the recorded cases, `memory-update` included (G08). Differences:
  - The linter-modified flush hashes its files under the session lock (TS's `readFileSync` blocks its event loop; same order), and the PostToolUse hash is read on tokio's blocking pool.
  - The turn-end `todos-current` reminder is queued under the lock that ends the turn, but the all-done / no-progress `clearTodos` still runs just after it, so a prompt racing that clear can still name the old list in `stale-todowrite`.
  - Date-change isn't exercised end to end: there is no clock seam, as in TS.
- Spinner tips (S22): at parity on 123 of 127 recorded cases (`tests/spinner_tips_golden.rs`). A `tipsFile` that serde_json refuses but `JSON.parse` accepts is ignored, so only the inline tips apply: a lone-surrogate escape, a number past f64, or nesting past 128 levels. A settings.json tip with `priority: 1e400` loses its priority (`json_parse` reads it as `null`); JS reads Infinity, sorts it first and prints `priority: null`.
- Nudges (S20): at parity on the recorded cases. `activeProfileExpiresAt` has no source until account pinning is wired (S30; the store and `AccountProfile::expires_at` are ported, G02a): the session value is always `None`, so `token_expiring_required` never fires today (the check and its fire-once flag are in place). `noteAccountAutoRotateObservation` is S30. Differences: `enable1mContext` is read as `== Some(true)` where TS tests truthiness (unreachable; the create route refuses a non-boolean with 400); TS's `isFinite` check is dropped as redundant with the range test.
- MCP notices (S15b): at parity on the recorded cases (`tests/mcp_notices.rs`). Differences: the checks run on their own tokio task, and the sync's SQLite transaction blocks a worker thread briefly (TS: one thread, synchronous better-sqlite3). Two replies that land together announce needs-auth first, as TS's microtask order does. One clock stamps both the 30 s window and `notified_at`; TS calls `Date.now` in each. As in TS, a cwd whose database can't open announces its servers again on every due check. TS's `isReplayingTranscript` guard on the re-check has no counterpart, because only live messages reach `on_message`.
- Thinking-replay recovery (S19): at parity on the recorded cases. Differences: an assistant record whose `uuid` is a truthy non-string (the CLI always writes a string) is passed over where TS would resume at it; the "(gone)" and create-failure messages aren't exercised by a test. The rebuild runs on a tokio task after the error is broadcast, as TS's fire-and-forget promise does. Closed (I14b): an unreadable transcript is none, as in the SDK, whose read catches (0.3.312 answers `[]` for a mode-0 file, recorded), so both report no safe rewind point.
- `POST /api/sessions/rename` (R05): a non-string `sessionId` is 400, and a non-string `dir` reads as none. TS hands both to the SDK (`Invalid sessionId: 5`, `e.normalize is not a function`). Pinned as KNOWN in `claudius-server/tests/cc2309_rename.rs`.
- Dead-session restart (R03): at parity on the recorded cases. Differences:
  - The rebuild runs on its own tokio task, so a dropped request can't leave the id unbound (TS promises can't be cancelled).
  - `close_streams` ends every subscriber. Only the stream route subscribes, so this ends exactly what TS closes.
  - The recorder doesn't pin a replayed `system/init`. The fake CLI writes no transcript, and Rust's replay never reaches `on_message`, so `came_up_live` can't count one.
- JSONL watcher start (S01b): the OS watch starts off-thread, as libuv starts `fs.watch`'s FSEvents stream, so `start()` returns at once; an external write in the seconds before the stream is up isn't seen live (a subscribe resyncs from disk).
- Account pinning (S30): ported and wired (`account_pinning.rs`); closed 2026-11-01. Was: `resolveAccountProfile`, `moveToActiveAccount` and `noteAccountAutoRotateObservation` weren't wired, so sessions spawned with the parent env. The drift refresh is ported (G02c: `account_drift`, `Session::set_account_pin`, run by `PATCH /api/accounts`), but no session is pinned yet, so it never fires. What they sit on is ported (G02a): the store, `build_env_for_profile`, `AccountProfile::expires_at` and `rotate_to_next_profile`. Under `CLAUDIUS_STATE_HOME`, `AccountsStore::from_env()` mirrors the state home's `.claude`; if the CLI should see the real one, pass the real home to `AccountsStore::new`.
- Notifications bus (G01a): the core is ported (`notification_bus.rs`) and the routes and SSE call it (G01b, G01c); sessions feed it through `session_notify.rs` (S31), and so does `dev-emit`. Differences, recorded or listed:
  - A number bound into a TEXT column (an odd-typed event field) is stored as rusqlite's bundled SQLite 3.50.2 prints it, which for some REALs has fewer digits than better-sqlite3's 3.53.2: `1/3` is `0.333333333333333` vs `0.33333333333333332`, and `2^53+2` is `9.00719925474099e+15` vs `9007199254740994.0`.
  - A `firstLine` cut through a surrogate pair stores U+FFFD, and the event carries U+FFFD. TS keeps the lone surrogate: it stores 3 WTF-8 bytes (read back as 3 U+FFFD) and the event carries `\ud83d`.
  - A workspaces.json entry whose `id` isn't a string is skipped. TS uses a number id as is, and a non-object entry gives `getAllWorkspaceStates` an `"undefined"` key.
  - `getAllWorkspaceStates` keys follow workspace order; TS's follow the order its reads settle.
  - Rows whose `createdAt` reads as NaN (non-numeric foreign text) compare equal in the cross-workspace sort, where V8's TimSort may order them differently.
  - A state flush whose read fails is logged, where TS throws out of its timer callback.
  - The bus reads workspaces.json and SQLite synchronously (TS awaits them fire-and-forget). S31 keeps that I/O off the session's thread: each session's calls queue in order and run on tokio's blocking pool.
  - The HMR singleton migration (`pickBus`, the build tag, the Proxy) isn't ported.
- Session → bus (S31): matches the TS on all 122 recorded steps. Differences:
  - One session's bus calls run strictly in order. TS's `void` calls interleave at their awaits: a same-tick answer can be marked read before its row lands, and the synchronous `markUserInput` is seen by a record still awaiting its lookup.
  - Events the bus can't act on aren't queued; TS only refreshes the cwd map for them.
  - `markUserInput` stamps the time when the queue runs it.
- Notification routes + SSE (G01b, G01c): at parity on every recorded step. Not mirrored: a number workspace id is found but the bus can't address it (list `[]`, `changed: 0`); dev-emit's non-string `sessionId` is `String(v)`; lone surrogates in dev-emit's `sessionId` / event and read-by-session's `sessionId` become U+FFFD (TS: WTF-8 bytes; a toast's event keeps the lone surrogate); a body number past f64 is `null`; with a `null` store entry TS's trusted-cwd check throws first, while here a string `cwd` is 400 `unknown cwd` and a non-string one fails in the states after bumping versions; the late group of the settle order is in workspace order (TS: I/O order); dev-emit's flush can beat its states under load (one version lower on that `state` event); the stream seeds and subscribes before answering (TS can lose events in between).
- Usage accounting (S17, fold fix 2026-10-27): matches the real `seedUsageBaseline()`, `foldResultIntoSessionUsage` and `lastRecordedCostState` on every recorded case, row included (`tests/usage_estimate_golden.rs`). The earlier non-string-model / non-numeric-token gaps are closed: the TS estimator now reads `(unknown)` and 0 for them too.
  - Closed (I27): `estimateSessionUsageFromTranscripts` and `scanFile` skip a file over node's 2 GiB `readFile` limit, or whose text passes V8's string limit, in both backends; Rust reads through `session_search::Limits::read_utf8`. Not recordable (512 MiB+ transcripts), so it is unit-tested with shrunk limits. Still different: TS's `scanFile failed` warning carries node's stack and the error's properties on further lines; Rust logs the first line (`String(err)`).
  - Closed (I29): `summarizeFile` gives a transcript past node's 2 GiB `readFile` limit or V8's string limit `firstSeenMs = 0` and no rows, cached by `(mtimeMs, size)`, and `readCache` treats such a cache as fresh, in both backends; Rust reads through `session_search::Limits::read_utf8`. Unit-tested with shrunk limits. Still different: past V8's limit TS's `writeCache` `JSON.stringify` throws (caught) and writes nothing, while Rust writes the cache and then reads it as fresh (cache churn only, same answers).
  - `lastRecordedCostState` streams as TS does (I14b): a line past V8's limit, or a read failing part-way, is `null` in both (unit-tested on `last_in`). Not recorded: a lone-surrogate escape in a `modelUsage` key reads as U+FFFD (`js::json_parse`); TS writes the `\udXXX` escape back.
- Start (S27): `start()` matches the real TS on all 87 recorded starts (`tests/session_start_golden.rs`). Not ported: account env and `ready.account` (S30). Differences: `canUseTool` / elicitation / PreToolUse text (S27b); `projectConfigRootFor`'s `git worktree list` runs synchronously inside `start()` (TS awaits `execFile`), blocking a tokio worker meanwhile. After a failed spawn Rust skips the auto-mode `supportedModels` check, which rejects and is caught in TS, so nothing observable differs (S27a). `findResumedTranscriptPath` also runs synchronously in `start()` (R04).
- Create body (S27d): matches the TS route on 35 of the 36 recorded bodies. The difference: `fallbackModel` is compared with `model` on their printed text, so `model: 5` + `fallbackModel: "5"` is a 500 here. TS's SDK compares the raw values (`5 !== "5"`) and starts with both flags.
- Stream (S28): `broadcast()`, `captureSnapshotState()`, `captureTaskState()` and `consume()` match the real TS on 33 recorded broadcast cases (306 steps) and 55 fake-CLI prompts (`session/tests/stream_golden.rs`, `tests/session_consume_golden.rs`).
  - Not ported: init `pluginLoadErrors` (G04), `noteAccountAutoRotateObservation` (S30).
  - Where TS throws out of `broadcast()`, Rust carries on and fans the event out: an `sdk` event whose `message` is null or missing, or a `commands_changed` whose `commands` isn't an array. Only `dev-broadcast` can send either.
  - A `get_usage` reply without `response` reads as `{}`, giving `plan_usage` with `rateLimits: null`. The SDK hands TS `undefined`, whose rejection sends nothing. The CLI always sends a `response`.
- Input (S29): `sendInput()`, `subscribe()`, the queue sends, the idle reaper, `lastRecordedPermissionMode` and `GET /api/sessions` match the real TS on all 173 recorded steps (since S29a), all 34 reap windows and all 26 permission-mode transcripts (`claudius-server/tests/session_input_golden.rs`).
  - Not recorded at the route (S29a): a slash send with a non-string `text`, where TS throws `text.trim is not a function` after marking the turn running, as Rust does (tested in `session_fake`); and a number `uuid` whose JS text isn't serde_json's (`1e21`) in the replies, which are `js::stringify` (the store recording covers `1e21`).
  - A queued row whose uuid is NULL (a posted `[null]` with `forceQueue`) can't be popped by uuid. `popHead` returns it without deleting it, and the in-memory queue keeps it (`[null] !== null`). Both are recorded at the store level, and Rust matches. By those rules the drain would send the row again at every idle. The session-level loop isn't recorded.
  - Not ported: `notificationBus.markUserInput` (S31); ultrathink and `transcriptPath` are R04's.
  - Not recorded: a queued row whose images throw when `flushQueueIfIdle` sends it (TS has an unhandled rejection, Rust logs it) or through `queue/[uuid]/send-now` (both answer the empty 500, as the recorded send-all does).
- Prompt order (R04): a Read / Edit / Write / NotebookEdit ask parks only after its symlink lookup (TS: an async `lstat` / `realpath`; Rust: `spawn_blocking`), so its place among simultaneous asks is a race on both sides and isn't recorded.
- Mirrored TS quirk (R04): `findResumedTranscriptPath` looks under `homedir()/.claude/projects`, not `$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/projects` where the CLI writes, so under a profile's config dir `transcriptPath` is absent unless the home holds a copy.

**Session titles (S23)**
- `getSessionTitle` (the row, then the legacy `session-titles.json` with its row migration), `resolveSessionTitle`, the title reads in `start()` and `sendFreshTitle`, `setSessionTitle`, `Session.rename` and the SDK's `renameSession` match the TS on 414 recorded cases (`tests/session_titles_golden.rs`, `session_fake`). Differences:
  - A lone surrogate in a legacy title (a `\ud800` escape, or a string `titles` indexed through half of an astral character) is U+FFFD in Rust. TS returns the lone surrogate, and better-sqlite3 stores it as 3 WTF-8 bytes that read back as 3 U+FFFD.
  - A legacy file nested past 128 levels doesn't parse (serde_json), so its title is missed; V8 reads it.
  - A BLOB `title` (only a foreign writer makes one) fails the read in Rust, which then treats it as no row. TS gets a `Buffer`: a fresh session takes it as its title, a resumed one gets none, and `sendFreshTitle` throws before sending anything.
- `getSessionTitlesByCwd` is ported with its legacy shadow read (D21: `sessions_db::get_session_titles_by_cwd`; a truthy non-string or inherited legacy entry throws as in TS). `sessions/open-tabs` uses it; `sessions/all` (D19) and the Cost page (D18) still run on Next. `sessions/info/[id]` (D19) calls `getSessionTitle`, so it will get the fallback through `sessions_db::get_session_title`.
- `lib/server/session-titles.ts` (the old writer) has no importer, so it has no Rust counterpart. Nothing writes the legacy file on either side.

**Cron (S11b)**
- `cron.rs` matches `lib/shared/cron.ts` on 140 expressions × 15 start instants × 7 local zones (UTC, New York, Lisbon, Kolkata, Lord Howe's 30-minute DST, Chatham, São Paulo's abolished DST), 29 `H` forms × 6 pinned `Math.random()` values, and 109 `nextFireAfter` cases (the DST anchor cases of `cc-parity-2.1.299-schedule-dst`, the stalled-iterator fallback, and 37 `tz` spellings), each with and without its `tz` (`tests/cron_golden.rs`, recorded by `rust/scripts/cron-golden.mjs` under node@22 with `TZ` set per zone). A child process per zone replays them once more through `Zone::Local`.
- Not mirrored: `Intl`'s extra ICU zone IDs (`PST`, `AET`, `SystemV/…`) are unknown (`nextFireAfter` → `None`, TS resolves them); zone data comes from the system tzdb (2026c on this Mac) where Node uses ICU's (2026a in Node 22.23), so a zone whose rules changed between the two can differ; past jiff's ±9999-year range a named zone keeps the boundary offset, and iterating past the end of JS time (±8.64e15 ms) isn't mirrored (TS turns to NaN). `H` draws its seed from `RandomState`, not `Math.random()`, so it is as random but not the same draw. The parser's `strict`, `hashSeed`, `startDate` / `endDate`, `prev()` and `describeCron` (cronstrue, client-only) aren't ported: no server caller uses them.
- Callers: the keep-alive (S11c), the scheduler (G07a: `nextFireAfter` anchored on `lastRunAt`) and the schedule routes' `validateCron` (G07b, POST and PATCH).

**Scheduler (G07a)**
- `scheduler_store.rs` and `scheduler.rs` match the TS on 309 recorded steps (`tests/scheduler_golden.rs`). Differences:
  - A job id whose run files `path.join` would put outside the schedule root (`../../x`) is refused: `appendRun` throws, `updateRun` is a no-op, `listRuns` gives `[]`. TS reads and writes there. A lone-surrogate escape reads, and is written, as U+FFFD.
  - A non-string `cwd` is no cwd (Node throws on spawn), a non-string `prompt` is sent as its `String()`, and a non-number cost or token count counts as 0.
  - An `arm` that fails outside a caller is logged, where TS leaves an unhandled rejection. A failed boot doesn't stop the server. `shutdown()` (SIGTERM) is Rust-only.
  - On unless `--no-scheduler` / `CLAUDIUS_SCHEDULER=0`; TS boots unless `CLAUDIUS_RUST_SCHEDULER` is set.
  - `processStartedAt` is read once per process (`process_started_ms`), not once per boot. There is no `pickScheduler` HMR probe.
  - `record_on_bus` records a non-string job id as its `String()`, where TS binds it raw (nullish as NULL). `run_note_text("toString")` passes the string through, where TS's `NOTE_TEXT[note]` returns the `Object.prototype` member.
- `main.rs` sets `on_run_finished` to `record_on_bus` (R01). The run SSE (G07c) uses `subscribe_run` / `is_run_live` / `is_run_attached`. The routes (G07b) gate on `scheduler::enabled()` and use `scheduler().store()`.

**Scheduled loops (S11a)**
- `trackScheduledLoops` matches the TS on 7 recorded scenarios (73 steps: loops, pending entries, applied stops, attribution and the stored tick rows after each), the `loop_ticks` DB layer on 15 steps and `listLoopBreakdown` on hand-written rows (`tests/loops_golden.rs`). It runs on the live stream, the transcript replay and the JSONL resync, like TS; `end()` clears it.
- Not mirrored, all shapes the CLI never sends: where TS would throw (a `null` content block, a Cron*/ScheduleWakeup tool_use whose `input` is `null` or missing) the block is skipped; a tool_use or tool_result without a string id is skipped (TS keys it by the raw value); a non-numeric `usage` field counts as 0 (JS `+` would coerce it).
- The live tracker runs just before the broadcast rather than after it: neither reads the other's state.
- The idle reaper reads it (S11c). `GET /api/schedule/session-loops` (+ `cancel`) is G07c.

**Recap (S12)**
- `extract_transcript_tail` and `cap_recap` match the TS on 18 recorded buffers and 28 cap cases, and `generate_recap` matches `generateRecap` on 13 fake-CLI runs, wire included (`tests/recap_golden.rs`, recorded by `rust/scripts/recap-golden.mjs`, which runs the real `generateRecap` through a loader shim that only adds the fake CLI's path).
- Differences:
  - A UTF-16 cut through a surrogate pair (the tail's per-line cut, the 400-unit cap) leaves JS with a lone high surrogate that reaches the CLI prompt or the SSE event as `\udXXX`; Rust drops that one unit. The budget arithmetic still counts it.
  - A cancelled query is `RecapOutcome::Aborted`. TS never takes its `aborted` branch, since the SDK's abort errors aren't named `AbortError`, and reports `failed` with "Operation aborted" or "Claude Code process aborted by user"; `requestRecap` drops either because its signal is aborted, so nothing is broadcast on both sides.
  - The dedupe check and the slot claim straddle the settings read in both. TS awaits that read, so concurrent requests can both pass; the Rust read is synchronous, which narrows the window.
  - A newer recap superseding an in-flight one is ported but untested: the 60 s window refuses it unless the first query is still running after a minute.

**Keep-alive (S11c)**
- `is_loop_armed` matches `isLoopArmed` on 24 recorded loops (wake-ups with a missing, zero, fractional, negative or huge delay; cancelled and recurring ones; one-shot crons that are empty, invalid, never firing, leap-day, DST-gap and `@hourly`) at the edges of the tick grace, the 7-day cap, the fire time and the first fire, 373 checks in all, plus 6 `hasArmedLoop` lists (`tests/keepalive_golden.rs`, recorded by `rust/scripts/keepalive-golden.mjs` with `TZ=America/New_York`).
- The reaper's check runs outside the session's state lock, after the pending-prompt check, like TS. `SessionManager::reap_ms_override` is a test-only hook with no 5-second floor.

**Feedback nudge (S26)**
- Matches the real `maybeOfferFeedbackSurvey` on 221 of 225 recorded steps and `submitFeedback` on 15 recorded calls (`tests/feedback_survey_golden.rs`). Differences:
  - The nudge is broadcast in place. TS broadcasts it after an awaited settings read, so its event usually lands after the turn-end to-do sync's events and can land after a drained queued turn's first ones. Only the client banner reads it.
  - A user settings.json nested past 128 levels anywhere counts as `{}` (serde_json's limit), so the default rate applies where `JSON.parse` reads the file's rate.
  - The roll is the RandomState `Math.random` stand-in shared with `cron.rs`: as random, not V8's draws. The check and the stamp are one locked step; TS re-checks after the await, with the same recorded outcome.
  - `submit_feedback(…, None, …)` is TS's `undefined` surface (the default). TS would put a non-string surface on the wire as is, but no caller passes one. TS feature-detects `query.submitFeedback`, which SDK 0.3.312 always has. Its new `submitSurveyFeedback` has no TS caller, so `Query` has no builder for it.
- `/api/feedback` is ported (G10), so a nudge Rust raised can be submitted; `submitFeedback`'s result is `{forwarded, reason?}`, so the refusal reason reaches the banner.

**Accounts (G02a)**
- `accounts.rs`, `account_profile.rs` and `claude_global_config.rs` match the TS on 107 recorded cases (483 steps) and 210 pure cases (`tests/accounts_golden.rs`). Differences, each listed in the test with its reason:
  - A lone-surrogate escape in `accounts.json` reads as U+FFFD, and a rewrite stores U+FFFD where TS writes the escape back. A 4-unit secret preview that cuts a surrogate pair shows U+FFFD.
  - A file nested past 128 levels doesn't load (serde_json), so every call errors where V8 reads it.
  - A profile response number past f64 (`1e400`) reads as `null`: the stored email is kept and nothing is backfilled. TS keeps `Infinity`, answers `null` and backfills `null`.
  - Deliberate: an id whose config dir isn't strictly inside `<dir>/profiles/` (`""`, `.`, `..`, `../x`, …; only a hand-edited file has one) is never provisioned ("profile id escapes the profiles directory"), and deleting it leaves the disk alone. TS provisions it and `rm -rf`s it: `""` removes every profile's config dir, and `..` removes the whole store with `accounts.json`.
  - Writes go through one process-wide lock; TS's read-modify-write can lose a concurrent update.
  - The lookup's metadata backfill runs before it returns; TS fires it and moves on.
  - `build_env_for_profile` returns strings, the values `spawn` would make with `String(v)`; a parent env value that isn't UTF-8 is converted lossily.
  - `node_fs::mkdir_recursive` stops with ENOENT where Node's walk spins forever (a relative store path under a deleted cwd). Since I14c, the settings, skills, keybindings and agents writers walk the same way (their copies spun forever).
- Resolved in G02c: one `ProfileInfoResolver` lives in `AppState` over the shared `Arc<HttpsClient>`.
- Untested: an id containing NUL (Node's `ERR_INVALID_ARG_VALUE` wording differs) and Windows (type-checked only; error codes off unix are coarse). The UI labels `ACCOUNT_KIND_LABEL` / `BEDROCK_AUTH_LABEL` are client-side and not ported.

**Accounts OAuth (G02b)**
- `account_oauth.rs` and `POST /api/accounts/oauth` match the TS on 109 recorded cases (`tests/account_oauth_golden.rs`; 101 of them also on the real binary). Differences, each listed in the tests with its reason:
  - A lone-surrogate escape in the pasted code reaches the token endpoint as U+FFFD; TS sends `\ud800`.
  - `expires_in` past f64 (`1e400`) reads as `null`, so no `expiresAt` is stored; TS stores `null` (Infinity).
  - A request body nested past 128 levels is a 400 `invalid JSON`; V8 parses it.
  - On the wire (`HttpsClient` vs undici): no `connection: keep-alive`; `accept-encoding: identity` and nothing decoded (undici asks for `gzip, deflate`, plus `br` over HTTPS); header names in lower case (undici keeps `Content-Type`).
- Not recorded: certificates are checked against the OS trust store plus a process-env `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` bundle (I19; Node: its bundled Mozilla list plus that bundle); reqwest also reads `ALL_PROXY`, fails a `CONNECT` the proxy drops (undici retries) and sends a proxied plain-http request in absolute form (undici tunnels it); the fetch spec's bad-port list isn't mirrored (no caller fetches such a URL).
- The CC 2.1.309 sign-in hint (R08) says to start Claudius with `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`; since I19, `HttpsClient` loads that bundle as extra roots on top of the OS store (see **Extra CA roots (I19)**).
- After a sign-in the route drops the new id from `AppState::profile_info` (G02c). With real random ids that is never observable on the binary; the seam is tested in core.
- Pending flows live in process memory, as in TS: a restart drops them.

**Extra CA roots (I19)**
- Node's load matches on all 54 recorded bundles and values (`tests/fetch_client.rs`). Not recorded:
  - Rust takes a non-self-signed CA in the bundle as a trust anchor; Node (no `X509_V_FLAG_PARTIAL_CHAIN`) needs the chain to reach a self-signed root.
  - webpki's certificate parse stands in for `d2i_X509`, so DER that one accepts and the other refuses reads differently.
  - A `Proc-Type:` header is taken as `bad decrypt` without reading `DEK-Info`.
- Trailing bytes 0x80 and up are trimmed as OpenSSL does with a signed `char` (macOS, where it was recorded). Where `char` is unsigned (Linux arm64), Node keeps them.
- Node reads the bundle the first time `node:tls` loads (the recorded `never-loaded` case prints nothing). The binary reads it at boot, so it warns at boot even if no request follows.
- A non-UTF-8 path is shown lossily in the warning.
- If the platform verifier still refuses the parsed roots, Rust logs `[fetch] the NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS roots were refused (…); using the default roots only`; Node has no such path.
- Since I23 the dictation socket shares these roots when TS gives it no `ca`, so the bundle is read and warned about once for both. If the platform verifier refuses them, the socket logs `[voice] the NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS roots were refused (…); using the default roots only` and uses the OS store alone.

**Accounts routes (G02c)**
- `accounts`, `accounts/profile` and `account` match the TS on every recorded case (`claudius-server/tests/accounts_routes.rs`). Not recorded: a lone-surrogate escape in a body reads as U+FFFD, and a body nested past 128 levels is a 400 `invalid JSON` (serde_json); TS refreshes live sessions concurrently (`Promise.all`), Rust in turn (same events); the profile resolver's store dir is fixed at startup, the routes read it per request (the env doesn't change at runtime).
- A throw is logged as `[api/<route>] <message>` (only the store's error text); TS leaves it to Next's error print.
- `/:id/account` (GET/POST: pin info, `moveToActiveAccount`) stays S30, and so does everything that pins a session.

**Voice (D25)**
- Trust: the OS store plus the bundle. Node checks its bundled Mozilla list, and for a settings-only bundle TS replaces that list with `[...rootCertificates, pem]`, which leaves out the process roots, as Rust does. Without one, a real-environment `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` bundle is I19's once-per-process roots (I23), so it has I19's differences (see **Extra CA roots (I19)**).
- A TLS failure that isn't a trust, validity or name failure has rustls's wording, not OpenSSL's `write EPROTO …`. The chain-with-root, longer-chain, validity and DNS/CN wordings follow Node's source; they weren't recorded.
- Not ported: the Bun branch of `openUpstream`, `ws`'s `maxFragments` / buffered-chunk limits, permessage-deflate, subprotocols and redirects (TS turns them off or never uses them). `chunk` reads its body only up to the 1 MiB cap; TS reads it all, then answers the same 413.
- The golden's TLS worlds need `openssl` on PATH and have only been verified on macOS.

**Settings backup (D11)**
- An import id (URL segment) or a published file path that resolves outside its dir is refused: 404, skipped, or a no-op DELETE. TS `join`s it and reads, writes or unlinks outside (`..%2F..%2Fsettings` → `~/.claude/settings.json`).
- A bundled customization file at `src/` itself (`""`, `"."`), when `src/` doesn't exist yet, is refused. TS writes a file named `src`.
- Customizations restore in order. TS's `Promise.all` interleaves the `index.json` read-modify-writes, so only one record of a multi-customization bundle survives; that TS bug isn't mirrored. When several reads fail together, the first in order is the one thrown (log only).
- An array-`content` chunk that is an Array-like object or `{type:"Buffer",data}` is refused; Node's `Buffer.from` makes bytes of it.
- A lone surrogate in an imported bundle becomes U+FFFD. TS keeps it and writes `\udXXX` escapes into the session and store files (`updater.json` included). A lone surrogate in `updater.json`'s `remote` / `branch` exports as U+FFFD too (TS writes the escape). Since D27c an import's rewrite keeps the file's own lone surrogates, as TS does.
- A literal U+10F800..U+10FFFF character in a bundle's `remote` / `branch` lands in `updater.json` as a lone-surrogate escape (`js_string`'s stand-in range). An `updater.json` nested past 1 000 levels reads as the defaults (docker::Js `MAX_DEPTH`; V8 reads it), so the export carries the default settings and an import rewrites the file to them (by inspection).
- Throws log as `[api/<route>] <name>: <message>`; a customize-settings error's name logs as `Error`.

**Mirrored TS bugs** (ported as-is so the Rust side matches; fixing them is T08)
- `!` bash mode (S10):
